{};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_campaign(&self, campaign: &PlayCampaign) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaigns(id,name,owner,max_players) VALUES ({},{},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\n            sql_text(&campaign.owner),\n            campaign.max_players\n        ))\n        .map(|_| true)\n    }\n\n    fn update_play_campaign_document(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        owner: &str,\n        document: &PlayCampaignDocument,\n    ) -> Result<PlayCampaignDocumentUpdate, ()> {\n        let campaign_owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let campaign_owner = campaign_owner.trim();\n        if campaign_owner.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentUpdate::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        if decode_sql_text(campaign_owner)? != owner {\n            return Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentUpdate::Forbidden);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaign_documents(campaign_id,story,dm_notes) VALUES ({},{},{}) ON CONFLICT(campaign_id) DO UPDATE SET story = excluded.story, dm_notes = excluded.dm_notes;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(&document.story),\n            sql_text(&document.dm_notes),\n        ))?;\n        Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentUpdate::Updated)\n    }\n\n    fn play_campaign_document(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        actor: &str,\n        include_dm_notes: bool,\n    ) -> Result<PlayCampaignDocumentRead, ()> {\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let owner = owner.trim();\n        if owner.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentRead::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        if include_dm_notes && decode_sql_text(owner)? != actor {\n            return Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentRead::Forbidden);\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(story),hex(dm_notes) FROM play_campaign_documents WHERE campaign_id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let row = row.trim();\n        if row.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentRead::Found(PlayCampaignDocument {\n                story: String::new(),\n                dm_notes: String::new(),\n            }));\n        }\n        let mut fields = row.split('|');\n        let document = PlayCampaignDocument {\n            story: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?,\n            dm_notes: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?,\n        };\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        Ok(PlayCampaignDocumentRead::Found(document))\n    }\n\n    fn play_scene_owner(&self, campaign_id: &str, owner: &str) -> Result<Option<bool>, ()> {\n        let stored_owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let stored_owner = stored_owner.trim();\n        if stored_owner.is_empty() {\n            Ok(None)\n        } else {\n            Ok(Some(decode_sql_text(stored_owner)? == owner))\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_scene(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        owner: &str,\n        scene: &PlayScene,\n    ) -> Result<PlaySceneCreate, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, owner)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlaySceneCreate::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlaySceneCreate::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_scenes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(&scene.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlaySceneCreate::Duplicate);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaign_scenes(campaign_id,id,name,status) VALUES ({},{},{},'open');\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&scene.id), sql_text(&scene.name)\n        ))?;\n        Ok(PlaySceneCreate::Created(PlayScene {\n            id: scene.id.clone(),\n            name: scene.name.clone(),\n            status: \"open\".to_string(),\n        }))\n    }\n\n    fn enter_play_scene(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        owner: &str,\n        scene_id: &str,\n    ) -> Result<PlaySceneEnter, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, owner)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlaySceneEnter::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlaySceneEnter::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(name),status FROM play_campaign_scenes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(scene_id)\n        ))?;\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\n        let Some(name_hex) = fields.next().filter(|field| !field.is_empty()) else {\n            return Ok(PlaySceneEnter::UnknownScene);\n        };\n        let status = fields.next().ok_or(())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        if status != \"open\" {\n            return Ok(PlaySceneEnter::Closed);\n        }\n        let name = decode_sql_text(name_hex)?;\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaign_scene_state(campaign_id,current_scene_id) VALUES ({},{}) ON CONFLICT(campaign_id) DO UPDATE SET current_scene_id = excluded.current_scene_id;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(scene_id)\n        ))?;\n        self.append_play_system_event(campaign_id, \"scene\", owner, scene_id)?;\n        Ok(PlaySceneEnter::Entered {\n            id: scene_id.to_string(),\n            name,\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn close_play_scene(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        owner: &str,\n        scene_id: &str,\n    ) -> Result<PlaySceneClose, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, owner)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlaySceneClose::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlaySceneClose::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_scenes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(scene_id)\n        ))?;\n        if exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlaySceneClose::UnknownScene);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"UPDATE play_campaign_scenes SET status = 'closed' WHERE campaign_id = {} AND id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\n            sql_text(scene_id)\n        ))?;\n        Ok(PlaySceneClose::Closed {\n            id: scene_id.to_string(),\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn append_play_system_event(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        kind: &str,\n        actor: &str,\n        text: &str,\n    ) -> Result<(), ()> {\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO play_campaign_system_events(campaign_id,sequence,kind,actor,text) VALUES ({},COALESCE((SELECT MAX(sequence) + 1 FROM (SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_narrations WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_actions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_travel_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_rest_events WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_resolutions WHERE campaign_id = {} UNION ALL SELECT sequence FROM play_campaign_system_events WHERE campaign_id = {})),1),{},{},{});\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(kind), sql_text(actor), sql_text(text),\n        )).map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    fn current_play_scene(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayCurrentScene, ()> {\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let owner = owner.trim();\n        if owner.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(PlayCurrentScene::UnknownCampaign);\n        }\n        if decode_sql_text(owner)? != actor {\n            let member = self.sql(&format!(\n                \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\",\n                sql_text(campaign_id),\n                sql_text(actor)\n            ))?;\n            if member.trim().is_empty() {\n                return Ok(PlayCurrentScene::Forbidden);\n            }\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT hex(s.id),hex(s.name),s.status FROM play_campaign_scene_state state JOIN play_campaign_scenes s ON s.campaign_id = state.campaign_id AND s.id = state.current_scene_id WHERE state.campaign_id = {} AND s.status = 'open';\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\n        ))?;\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\n        let Some(id_hex) = fields.next().filter(|field| !field.is_empty()) else {\n            return Ok(PlayCurrentScene::Missing);\n        };\n        let scene = PlayScene {\n            id: decode_sql_text(id_hex)?,\n            name: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?,\n            status: fields.next().ok_or(())?.to_string(),\n        };\n        if fields.next().is_some() {\n            return Err(());\n        }\n        Ok(PlayCurrentScene::Found(scene))\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_location(\n        &self,\n        campaign_id: &str,\n        owner: &str,\n        location: &PlayLocation,\n    ) -> {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n        bytes.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);\n        if let Some(end) = bytes.windows(4).position(|w| w == b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\") {\n            header_end = end + 4;\n            break;\n        }\n        if bytes.len() > MAX_HEADER_BYTES {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n    }\n    let headers = match std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[..header_end]) WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?;\n                if member.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Forbidden); }\n            }\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(item_id),quantity,status,COALESCE(hex(recipient_character_id),''),COALESCE(votes,-1) FROM play_campaign_loot WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?;\n        let mut fields = row.trim().split('|');\n        let Some(item_hex) = fields.next().filter(|value| !value.is_empty()) else { return Ok(PlayLootResult::Missing); };\n        let item_id = decode_sql_text(item_hex)?;\n        let quantity = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        let status = fields.next().ok_or(())?.to_string();\n        let recipient_hex = fields.next().ok_or(())?;\n        let votes: i64 = fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n        let vote_tallies = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(recipient_character_id),COUNT(*) FROM play_campaign_loot_votes WHERE campaign_id = {} AND loot_id = {} GROUP BY recipient_character_id ORDER BY recipient_character_id ASC;\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(loot_id)))?\n            .lines()\n            .map(|line| {\n                let (recipient_hex, count) = line.split_once('|').ok_or(())?;\n                Ok((decode_sql_text(recipient_hex)?, count.parse().map_err(|_| ())?))\n            })\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<(String, i64)>, ()>>()?;\n        Ok(PlayLootResult::Found(PlayLoot { loot_id: loot_id.to_string(), item_id, quantity, status, recipient_character_id: (!recipient_hex.is_empty()).then(|| decode_sql_text(recipient_hex)).transpose()?, votes: (votes >= 0).then_some(votes), vote_tallies }))\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_agenda_npc(&self, campaign_id: self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(is_dm) => is_dm,\n        };\n        if !is_dm {\n            let return Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::Forbidden); }\n        }\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(name),hex(agenda),hex(public_status) FROM play_campaign_agenda_npcs WHERE campaign_id = {} AND decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)? };\n        if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n        Ok(PlayAgendaNpcResult::Found { npc, is_dm })\n    }\n\n    fn append_play_npc_dialogue(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, npc_id: &str, entry: &PlayNpcDialogue) -> Result<PlayNpcDialogueResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let npc = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_agenda_npcs WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id)))?;\n        if npc.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Missing); }\n        let existing = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_npc_dialogue WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {} AND dialogue_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id), sql_text(&entry.dialogue_id)))?;\n        if !existing.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Conflict); }\n        let position: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM play_campaign_npc_dialogue WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        self.sql(&format!(\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_npc_dialogue(campaign_id,npc_id,dialogue_id,speaker,text,visibility,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{},{},{});\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id), sql_text(&entry.dialogue_id), sql_text(&entry.speaker), sql_text(&entry.text), sql_text(&entry.visibility), position))?;\n        Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Created(PlayNpcDialogue { dialogue_id: entry.dialogue_id.clone(), speaker: entry.speaker.clone(), text: entry.text.clone(), visibility: entry.visibility.clone() }))\n    }\n\n    fn play_npc_dialogue(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, npc_id: &str) -> Result<PlayNpcDialogueResult, ()> {\n        let is_dm = match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(is_dm) => is_dm,\n        };\n        if !is_dm {\n            let member = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?;\n            if member.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Forbidden); }\n        }\n        let npc = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_agenda_npcs WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id)))?;\n        if npc.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::Missing); }\n        let visibility_filter = if is_dm { \"\" } else { \" AND visibility = 'public'\" };\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(dialogue_id),hex(speaker),hex(text),visibility FROM play_campaign_npc_dialogue WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {}{} ORDER BY position ASC;\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(npc_id), visibility_filter))?;\n        let entries = rows.lines().map(|line| {\n            let mut fields = line.split('|');\n            let dialogue_id = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let speaker = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let text = decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?;\n            let visibility = fields.next().ok_or(())?.to_string();\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            Ok(PlayNpcDialogue { dialogue_id, speaker, text, visibility })\n        }).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>()?;\n        Ok(PlayNpcDialogueResult::History(entries))\n    }\n\n    fn play_relationship_entity_exists(&self, campaign_id: &str, entity_id: &str) -> Result<bool, ()> {\n        let row = self.sql(&format!(\n            \"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {} UNION SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_agenda_npcs WHERE campaign_id = {} AND npc_id = {} LIMIT 1;\",\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(entity_id), sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(entity_id)\n        ))?;\n        Ok(!row.trim().is_empty())\n    }\n\n    fn create_play_relationship(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, edge: &PlayRelationship) -> Result<PlayRelationshipResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        if !self.play_relationship_entity_exists(campaign_id, &edge.source_id)? || !self.play_relationship_entity_exists(campaign_id, &edge.target_id)? {\n            return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Missing);\n        }\n        let existing = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_relationships WHERE campaign_id = {} AND source_id = {} AND target_id = {} AND kind = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&edge.source_id), sql_text(&edge.target_id), sql_text(&edge.kind)))?;\n        if !existing.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Conflict); }\n        let position: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM play_campaign_relationships WHERE campaign_id = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\n        self.sql(&format!(\"INSERT INTO play_campaign_relationships(campaign_id,source_id,target_id,kind,score,position) VALUES ({},{},{},{},{},{});\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(&edge.source_id), sql_text(&edge.target_id), sql_text(&edge.kind), edge.score, position))?;\n        Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Created(PlayRelationship { source_id: edge.source_id.clone(), target_id: edge.target_id.clone(), kind: edge.kind.clone(), score: edge.score }))\n    }\n\n    fn update_play_relationship(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, source_id: &str, target_id: &str, kind: &str, score: i64) -> Result<PlayRelationshipResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(false) => return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Forbidden),\n            Some(true) => {}\n        }\n        let exists = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_relationships WHERE campaign_id = {} AND source_id = {} AND target_id = {} AND kind = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(source_id), sql_text(target_id), sql_text(kind)))?;\n        if exists.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Missing); }\n        self.sql(&format!(\"UPDATE play_campaign_relationships SET score = {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND source_id = {} AND target_id = {} AND kind = {};\", score, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(source_id), sql_text(target_id), sql_text(kind)))?;\n        Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Updated(PlayRelationship { source_id: source_id.to_string(), target_id: target_id.to_string(), kind: kind.to_string(), score }))\n    }\n\n    fn play_relationships(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str) -> Result<PlayRelationshipResult, ()> {\n        let is_dm = match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n            None => return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::UnknownCampaign),\n            Some(value) => value,\n        };\n        if !is_dm {\n            let member = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(actor)))?;\n            if member.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Forbidden); }\n        }\n        let rows = self.sql(&format!(\"SELECT hex(source_id),hex(target_id),hex(kind),score FROM play_campaign_relationships WHERE campaign_id = {} ORDER BY position ASC;\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?;\n        let edges = rows.lines().map(|line| {\n            let mut fields = line.split('|');\n            let edge = PlayRelationship { source_id: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?, target_id: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?, kind: decode_sql_text(fields.next().ok_or(())?)?, score: fields.next().ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())? };\n            if fields.next().is_some() { return Err(()); }\n            Ok(edge)\n        }).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, ()>>()?;\n        Ok(PlayRelationshipResult::Listed(edges))\n    }\n\n change_play_faction_reputation(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, faction_id: &str, character_id: &str, delta: i64, reason: &str) -> Result<PlayFactionResult, ()> {\n        match self.play_scene_owner(campaign_id, actor)? {\n                Err(PlayMemberError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(PlayMemberError::UnknownCampaign) => {\n                    respond(stream, 404, r#\"{\"error\":\"not found\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(PlayMemberError::Conflict) => respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"conflict\"}\"#),\n                Err(PlayMemberError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if path == \"/v1/play/campaigns\" {\n            let Some((actor_name, actor_role)) =\n                authenticated_user(authorization.as_deref(), users)\n            else {\n                return respond(stream, 401, r#\"{\"error\":\"unauthorized\"}\"#);\n            };\n            if actor_role != \"dm\" {\n                return respond(stream, 403, r#\"{\"error\":\"forbidden\"}\"#);\n            }\n            return match create_play_campaign(&body, actor_name, storage) {\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 201, &response),\n                Err(PlayCampaignError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(PlayCampaignError::Duplicate) => {\n                    respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate id\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(PlayCampaignError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if path == \"/v1/storage/reset\" {\n            return match storage.reset() {\n                Ok(()) => {\n                    sessions.clear();\n                    users.clear();\n                    respond(stream, 200, r#\"{\"ok\":true,\"schema_version\":1}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(()) => respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#),\n            };\n        }\n        if path == Err(CompendiumError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CompendiumError::Duplicate) => {\n                    respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate slug\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CompendiumError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if path == \"/v1/compendium/items\" {\n            return match create_item(&body, storage) {\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 201, &response),\n                Err(CompendiumError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CompendiumError::Duplicate) => {\n                    respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate slug\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CompendiumError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if path == \"/v1/campaigns\" {\n            return match create_campaign(&body, storage) {\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 201, &response),\n                Err(CampaignError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CampaignError::Duplicate) => {\n                    respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate id\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CampaignError::UnknownCampaign) => unreachable!(),\n                Err(CampaignError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if let Some(id) = path\n            .strip_prefix(\"/v1/campaigns/\")\n            .and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\"/analytics/risk-report\"))\n        {\n            return match campaign_risk_report(id, &body, storage) {\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 200, &response),\n                Err(AnalyticsError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(AnalyticsError::UnknownCampaign) =>       respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate id\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CampaignError::UnknownCampaign) => {\n                    respond(stream, 404, r#\"{\"error\":\"not found\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(CampaignError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if let Some(id) = path\n            .strip_prefix(\"/v1/campaigns/\")\n            .and_then(|p| p.strip_suffix(\"/events\"))\n        {\n            return        }\n                Err(SessionError::Duplicate) => respond(stream, 409, r#\"{\"error\":\"duplicate id\"}\"#),\n                Err(SessionError::UnknownCampaign | SessionError::UnknownSession) Err(SessionError::Storage) => {\n                    respond(stream, 500, r#\"{\"error\":\"storage failure\"}\"#)\n                }\n            };\n        }\n        if let Some((campaign_id, {\n            return match advance_crafting_project(campaign_id, project_id, &body, storage) {\n                Ok(response) => respond(stream, 200, &response),\n                Err(CraftingError::Invalid) => {\n                    respond(stream, 400, r#\"{\"error\":\"invalid request\"}\"#)\n                }\n                Err(\n {};\",\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\n        ))?;\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n        self.sql(&format!(\n            \"INSERT INTO
